Our preliminary experiments reveal that optimal memory weight varies with resolution and viscosity, implying that a fixed memory weight cannot simultaneously optimize performance across diverse settings. We propose AMGFNO, which dynamically modulates memory weight through a learnable gate. On the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky and Burgers' equations, AMGFNO achieves 55-79% nRMSE reduction over at low resolution, with the learned gate value automatically decreasing from $\bar{g} \approx 0.7$ to near-zero as resolution increases.

</details>


### [116] [Clustering Node Attributed Networks with Graph Neural Networks and Self Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13444)
*Rodrigo de Sapienza Luna,Daniel Ratton Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的无监督图聚类框架，通过多轮自学习迭代，利用原始图构建的上下文图生成节点表示，并利用聚类结果影响下一轮图结构。实验表明该方法在合成数据上优于仅依赖网络或属性特征的算法，在真实数据集上当聚类平衡时与最先进方法竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图聚类方法通常只利用网络边信息或节点属性信息之一，而真实场景中节点属性往往也包含重要信息。现有方法缺乏一种有效融合网络结构与节点属性、并在完全无监督设置下进行自学习的统一框架。

Method: 提出多轮自学习框架：每轮使用GNN生成节点表示并进行聚类，聚类结果影响下一轮使用的图结构；同时每轮基于原始图构建上下文图用于生成节点表示。整个过程在完全无监督设置下进行。

Result: 合成数据上，该方法同时从边和属性中提取信息，当两者信息量都不高时优于仅关注网络或属性的算法；多轮学习优于单轮长训练；真实数据上，当聚类大小平衡时与最先进方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 该方法有效融合了图结构和节点属性信息，且多轮迭代自学习策略比传统单轮GNN聚类表现更好，但在聚类不平衡的真实场景中仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Graph clustering - partitioning the node set of a graph into disjoint subsets that reflect some latent information - is a fundamental problem as it finds applications in a myriad of different scenarios. While this classic problem has been tackled for decades by different communities, a recent variation of the problem driven by real data considers the scenario where nodes have attributes that are also informative. This has triggered novel methods that simultaneously leverage network information (edges) and node information (attributed) in the design of novel clustering algorithms. This work proposes a novel framework that builds on prior works that have applied graph neural networks (GNN) to graph clustering. The proposed framework operates in rounds of self learning in a fully unsupervised setting. In each round, a GNN generates representations for nodes that are used to cluster the nodes. This clustering influences the graph used to generate the node representation in the next round. Moreover, a context graph built in each round using the original graph is used to generate the node representations. Empirical results show that the proposed methodology extracts information from both network edges and node attributes in synthetic data, outperforming algorithms focused solely on the network or attributes when neither are very informative. Multiple rounds of learning also improve the performance and always outperforms a long single round of training (i.e., classic GNN graph clustering). When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [117] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后验方法，利用Laplace近似估计预训练分子扩散模型生成样本的不确定性分数，该分数与样本质量负相关，可用于过滤低质量样本以提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D分子生成中被广泛采用，但缺乏一种原则性的信号来指示生成的分子何时可能是低质量的。

Method: 基于去噪网络的Laplace近似，测量整个生成轨迹中噪声预测的可变性，从而得到每个样本的不确定性分数。

Result: 不确定性分数与样本级质量指标呈负相关，表明其对样本质量具有信息性；该分数可用于过滤生成样本，通过测试时缩放提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 所提的不确定性估计方法能够有效识别低质量分子生成样本，并且可以作为后处理手段提升预训练扩散模型的整体生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [118] [Reinforcement Learning for Neural Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13461)
*Shaivi Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个探索性框架，将神经模型编辑形式化为强化学习问题，通过奖励反馈学习编辑策略，在偏见消除和机器遗忘任务上取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络编辑需要针对特定目标设计专门算法，耗时耗力。作者希望提出一种通用框架，使编辑策略能从奖励反馈中学习，而非为每个任务手动设计算法。

Method: 将神经模型编辑建模为强化学习问题，设计了两个环境：MaskWorld（乘法缩放权重）和ShiftWorld（加法更新权重）。奖励函数结合了效用保持目标和任务特定编辑目标。在文本分类的偏见消除和图像分类的机器遗忘任务上评估。

Result: 在机器遗忘任务中，遗忘集准确率降至近0%，保留集准确率保持90%以上；在偏见消除任务中，偏见相关性能提升超过5%，同时保持通用分类能力。

Conclusion: 神经模型编辑可以形式化为强化学习问题，编辑策略可从奖励反馈中学习，无需为每个任务手动设计专门算法。

Abstract: Editing pretrained neural networks requires specialized algorithms tailored to specific objectives. Designing such algorithms is often time-consuming and demands significant effort. We present an exploratory framework that formulates neural model editing as a reinforcement learning problem, where agents modify models using reward feedback. We introduce two environments: MaskWorld, where agents scale weights multiplicatively, and ShiftWorld, where agents apply additive weight updates. The reward function combines a utility-preservation objective with a task-specific editing objective, enabling agents to learn targeted modifications while maintaining overall model performance. We evaluate the framework on bias mitigation in text classification and machine unlearning in image classification, both of which traditionally rely on specialized algorithms. Our results show that the learned policies reduce forget set accuracy to nearly 0% while preserving over 90% retain set accuracy on the unlearning task. In the bias mitigation setting, the learned policies improve bias-related performance by more than 5% while maintaining general classification utility. Our findings show that neural model editing can be cast as a reinforcement learning problem, allowing editing policies to be learned from reward feedback rather than manually engineered for each task.

</details>


### [119] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaxProof是一种面向竞赛级数学证明的总体测试时扩展框架，通过将M3模型的证明生成、验证和修正能力融合，结合种群级搜索和锦标赛选择，在IMO 2025和USAMO 2026上分别达到35/42和36/42，超越人类金牌线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决竞赛级数学证明中需要高可靠性的难题，现有方法在证明验证环节存在高假阳性率，且缺乏高效的测试时扩展机制来提升证明质量。

Method: 首先训练三种面向证明的能力（证明生成、证明验证、基于批判的证明修复），采用纵深防御生成式验证器实现低假阳性率；将这些能力合并到单一M3模型中；测试时，模型作为生成器、验证器、优化器和排序器，在候选证明种群中进行搜索，通过锦标赛选择返回最终证明。

Result: M3模型在IMO 2025上达到35/42，在USAMO 2026上达到36/42，均超过人类金牌阈值。

Conclusion: MaxProof证明了种群级测试时扩展策略能显著提升竞赛级别数学证明的性能，并已超越人类顶尖水平。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [120] [SupraBench: A Benchmark for Supramolecular Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13477)
*Tianyi Ma,Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Ziming Li,Connor R. Schmidt,Chuxu Zhang,Matthew J. Webber,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SupraBench是首个用于评估大语言模型在超分子化学推理能力的基准测试，包含4个核心任务和1个辅助视觉任务，附带16M token的领域语料库SupraPMC。实验表明现有LLM在所有任务上均有显著提升空间，领域适应预训练对分布内回归有效但会牺牲严格格式输出，且不同任务族的难度差异揭示了超分子化学推理中的特定能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前设计主客体系统耗时耗力（每个候选对需数天验证），虽然LLM在分子结合任务中表现优异，但缺乏系统性评估LLM在超分子化学推理能力上的标准基准。

Method: 与领域专家合作发布SupraBench基准，设计4个核心任务（结合亲和力预测、最优结合剂选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述）和1个视觉辅助任务（分子识别），同时构建16M token的SupraPMC语料库用于领域适应预训练。

Result: 现有开源和闭源LLM在所有任务上均有显著提升空间；领域适应预训练对分布内回归任务有效但会牺牲严格格式输出；不同任务族的难度差异揭示了当前超分子化学推理中的特定能力缺口。

Conclusion: SupraBench为超分子化学领域的LLM推理能力评估提供了首个标准化基准，揭示了现有模型在该领域的局限性，并为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Supramolecular chemistry, which includes the study of non-covalent host-guest assemblies, has advanced various applications. However, designing host-guest systems remains time-consuming, requiring days of dry-lab verification per candidate pair. Although LLMs have emerged as a fast alternative with strong performance on molecular binding tasks, no benchmark currently systematically evaluates LLMs for host-guest reasoning across fundamental supramolecular chemistry tasks, e.g., binding affinity prediction. To this end, we collaborate with domain experts to release the first Supramolecular Benchmark, called SupraBench, to evaluate LLMs in chemistry reasoning. Specifically, we design four fundamental tasks, i.e., binding affinity prediction, top-binder selection, solvent identification, and host-guest description, plus an auxiliary vision-based task for molecular identification. We also release SupraPMC, a curated 16M-token corpus of Supramolecular chemistry articles distilled from Europe PMC, to support the adaptation to the supramolecular domain. We benchmark a broad range of open and proprietary LLMs and find that LLMs leave substantial headroom across all tasks. Domain adaptation pretraining over SupraPMC transfers cleanly to in-distribution regression but trades off against strict letter-format output. Moreover, the difficulty profile differs sharply across task families, revealing distinct failure modes that indicate specific gaps in current supramolecular chemistry reasoning. Our source codes and benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/Tianyi-Billy-Ma/SupraBench.

</details>


### [121] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTIIF是一种完全无监督的多变量时间序列异常检测框架，通过生成500个跨四种小波族的随机解析特征，分别针对点异常、分布异常、时间异常和集体异常四种类型，使用五个结构化孤立森林（每种异常类型一个，外加一个元森林处理复合异常），并采用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值校准。在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上，平均F1=0.228（全部数据集）和0.322（13个可检测数据集），VUS-PR排名第一（0.463，相比之前最佳0.329提升40.7%）。消融实验证实自适应阈值（+38%）、四分支结构（+20%）和元森林（+23%）均至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列中的异常类型在结构上存在四种不同类型（点异常、分布异常、时间异常和集体异常），每种类型需要不同的特征表示。现有无监督方法大多只针对其中一种或两种类型，且可解释性有限，缺乏一个能统一处理所有四种类型且无需数据集特定调参的框架。

Method: CRAFTIIF生成K=500个跨四个小波族（Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet）的随机解析小波特征，每个特征针对特定异常类型。构建五个结构化孤立森林（每个类型一个，外加一个元森林处理复合异常），使用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值校准检测，检测率范围从0.1%到69.2%。每个IF仅在类型特定特征上训练，分支触发直接提供异常类型归因。

Result: 在mTSBench所有19个数据集上，平均F1=0.228（全部）和0.322（13个可检测数据集），在25种评估方法中排名第一。VUS-PR达0.463，相比之前最佳0.329提升40.7%。诊断框架识别出19个数据集中的6个对任何无监督方法都基本不可检测。消融实验显示自适应阈值化（+38% F1）、四分支结构（+20%）和元森林（+23%）均不可或缺。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF首次在完全无监督且无需数据集调参的条件下，统一处理了四种结构不同的异常类型，并在大规模基准测试中取得领先性能。其内置的可解释性通过分支归因直接实现，无需事后解释。研究同时揭示了部分数据集对无监督方法的根本不可检测性。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [122] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A2D2框架，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略及基于质量的推理调度，实现对任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调，并理论上保证收敛到奖励倾斜分布，实验证明其在奖励优化和生成质量上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在序列生成中表现出色，但针对任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调尚未得到充分探索，现有方法局限于固定长度设置或仅使用推理时引导。

Method: 推导了联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，实现无需目标样本的理论保证收敛；提出自适应联合解码（AJD）损失，联合优化插入和去掩码策略与基于质量的推理调度。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性和准确性上均优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调提供了统一的理论框架和实用方法，在奖励优化和生成质量上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [123] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种调整杯积神经层（adjusted cup product neural layer），该层通过硬编码杯积（cup product）与高规范理论的调整项，实现了规范不变的读出机制。理论结果表明，在闭链上输出完全取决于调整系数，调整是规范不变信号的唯一来源，且该可观测量为非零二次型，在单次和两次规范变换下严格不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理和几何中许多重要可观测量是余链的杯积，但现有神经网络方法未能保证规范不变性。本文旨在设计一种内在具有规范不变性的神经层，以可靠地提取物理不变量。

Method: 提出调整杯积神经层，将杯积与高规范理论中的调整项结合为神经原语，强制实现规范不变的读出机制。

Result: 在闭链上，输出完全由调整系数决定；调整系数为零时输出归零；该可观测量是非零二次型，且在单次和两次规范变换下严格不变。

Conclusion: 调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源，所提结构为物理和几何中规范不变可观测量的神经网络学习提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [124] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies提出了一个统一框架，将时间序列预测重构为未来可观测性推断与值估计的联合问题，通过观测流和值流双流架构及三个专用模块建模两者的交互，并在包含自然缺失数据和工业数据的Shadow基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间模型（如Neural ODEs）虽然改进了对历史不规则的建模，但在推理时仍隐式假设未来有效观测的时间戳是预先已知的，这在许多实际系统中不成立——不仅要预测未来值，还要判断未来是否会发生有效观测。

Method: Timeflies采用双流架构：观测流和值流，通过三个专用模块耦合——可靠性感知嵌入（reliability-aware embedding）、观测引导的依赖建模（observation-guided dependency modeling）和联合预测（joint prediction），将预测重构为未来可观测性推断与值估计的联合问题。

Result: 在Shadow基准（结合公共数据集自然缺失与工业数据）上，Timeflies一致优于现有方法，并引入了观测-值联合熵（OVJE）指标来综合评估耦合可预测性。

Conclusion: 显式建模未来可观测性对于含缺失值的时间序列预测至关重要。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [125] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB)的后训练压缩与概率校准框架，通过联合优化概率单纯形上的Out-Of-Bag损失并引入凹二次惩罚解决L1-单纯形悖论，实现了高达96%的集成压缩、线性推理加速和更好的概率校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Bagging集成（如随机森林、Bagged SVM、Bagged神经网络）对所有基估计器采用均匀投票权重，忽略了基估计器在不同局部的能力差异，导致模型过度自信。现有方法缺乏同时在概率校准和集成压缩两个目标上的理论严谨框架。

Method: 将集成剪枝与校准建模为概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，以最小化Out-Of-Bag损失为目标。为解决L1范数在单纯形上恒为常数、无法实现稀疏化的理论悖论，引入了凹二次惩罚项诱导稀疏性。该框架是模型无关的。

Result: 实现了高达96%的集成压缩率，线性推理加速，降低了期望校准误差（Expected Calibration Error），同时保持或提升了泛化准确性。

Conclusion: SCSB是一个理论严谨、模型无关的集成后处理框架，能够同时实现高效的集成压缩与概率校准，克服了朴素均匀权重的局限性。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现链式思维推理中存在一个"承诺边界"——模型从临时猜测到稳定高置信度答案的急剧转变点，通常发生在推理结束前的单个步骤中，此后的推理步骤对最终答案无实质影响（"附带现象"）。利用注意力探针可线性解码答案形成阶段，并据此提前退出推理，在几乎不影响性能的情况下将CoT长度平均减少55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理是推理时扩展的主流范式，但单个推理步骤对最终答案的因果影响机制尚不明确，理解这一过程对提升推理效率和可解释性至关重要。

Method: 通过早期退出（early exit）估计每个推理步骤的因果重要性；使用注意力探针（attention probes）从中间推理步骤中线性解码答案形成阶段，并基于承诺边界信号实现推理块的提前退出。

Result: 推理通常在一个单步中经历从临时猜测到稳定高置信度答案的急剧转变（承诺边界），此后的CoT步骤对最终答案概率无影响；注意力探针可高精度解码答案形成阶段，并泛化到未见过的推理任务；基于此提前退出可在几乎不影响性能的情况下将CoT长度平均减少55%。

Conclusion: 链式思维推理中大部分步骤是"附带现象"的，对最终答案无实质贡献；通过检测承诺边界可以实现高效的推理加速，且该信号具有良好的跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [127] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于Accessibility Collapse框架，研究了持续学习中的可恢复性几何结构。通过在Split CIFAR-100和ResNet-18上的实验，发现遗忘知识并非被破坏，而是仍在低维子空间（平均k_t=8.0）中保持紧凑可解码，支持了"稳定恢复流形假设"——灾难性遗忘本质是访问性和流形对齐问题，而非信息毁灭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将灾难性遗忘视为先前知识的破坏，但对其几何结构与可恢复性的理解不足。本文旨在通过几何视角，探究遗忘知识是否仍以某种结构形式存在，从而区分"信息破坏"与"访问性障碍"两种解释。

Method: 基于Accessibility Collapse框架，在Split CIFAR-100数据集上使用顺序训练的ResNet-18进行10个任务的持续学习实验。引入恢复子空间维度（k_t）作为度量，即保持全探针性能90%所需的最小奇异方向数。同时分析表征漂移、主角度漂移与可恢复性的关系，并建立几何模型解释可恢复性方差。

Result: 恢复维度在整个训练过程中保持稳定（平均k_t=8.0），拒绝了"可恢复性扩散"假设。主角度漂移与可恢复性高度负相关（r=-0.862），简单几何模型解释了82.2%的可恢复性方差。尽管存在显著的表征漂移，遗忘知识仍保持紧凑的解码结构。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘主要是访问性和流形对齐问题，而非信息毁灭。遗忘的知识仍然存在于低维子空间中，可通过适当机制恢复。这为设计基于流形对齐和访问性增强的持续学习算法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [128] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了On-policy Distillation (OPD)对模型参数的影响，发现其更新具有稀疏性和几何集中性：更新量小且坐标稀疏，集中于FFN层；数值上满秩但谱集中，主要落在源权重接近零的坐标上，表明OPD并非密集参数重写，而是保留了on-policy post-training的几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD作为一种结合on-policy学生轨迹和密集教师监督的微调方法近来受到关注，但其如何改变模型参数的内在机制尚不明确，因此需要对OPD更新的参数特性进行深入分析。

Method: 在多个语言和视觉-语言模型对上，从稀疏性和几何两个维度分析OPD参数更新的特征：稀疏性方面考察更新量大小、坐标稀疏性及层间分布；几何方面考察数值秩、谱集中性及与源权重的空间关系。

Result: 1) OPD更新具有稀疏性：更新量小、坐标稀疏，主要分布在FFN层，并且仅训练发现的子网络即可接近完整OPD性能；但稀疏诱导的SGD优化器不如AdamW，因为密集教师监督保留了异构梯度尺度。2) OPD更新数值满秩但谱集中，偏离源权重的奇异子空间，主要落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使OPD变成普通的密集参数重写，OPD保留了on-policy post-training的重要几何特征。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [129] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了截断位置编码（如前k个特征向量或邻接矩阵的幂）在图神经网络中的表达能力，发现截断后不同家族的位置编码在表达能力上存在根本性差异，截断的谱位置编码不再强于1-WL测试，并提出混合使用截断位置编码的实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然理论上谱位置编码和基于游走的位置编码在完整版本下表达能力等价（介于1-WL和3-WL之间），但实践中由于$O(n^3)$的时空复杂度，通常使用截断版本，而这些截断版本的理论性质尚不清楚。

Method: 理论分析截断后不同PE家族的表达能力差异；研究k-调和距离这一谱PE族的表达能力；在真实数据集上进行实验比较不同截断PE的组合效果。

Result: 截断后不同PE家族在表达能力上存在根本性差异；截断的谱PE不再强于1-WL测试；即使密切相关的截断PE（如k-调和距离）在表达能力上也有显著差异；混合使用截断PE在真实数据集上优于任何单一家族。

Conclusion: 完整版本下等价的PE在截断后表达能力不同，实践中应考虑混合使用多种截断PE以取得更好效果。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolSense诊断框架，用于评估基于参数化工具检索的LLM智能体在真实场景下的工具理解能力，发现模型存在知识-检索分离现象：在模糊查询下性能大幅下降（最高降64个百分点），且高检索性能不一定对应高工具知识理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化工具检索评估仅使用详尽明确的查询，且采用约束解码限制输出，无法揭示模型是否真正理解工具语义。因此需要更全面的诊断框架来评估模型在真实模糊查询场景下的表现。

Method: 提出ToolSense开源诊断框架，输入任意工具目录后可自动生成三类基准测试：(1) Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB)，包含三个模糊度等级的查询；(2) MCQ探测基准；(3) QA探测基准。应用于ToolBench（约4.7万工具）评估五种参数化模型训练配置。

Result: 在RRB模糊查询下，多种配置性能较完全明确查询的ToolBench基准下降50-64个百分点，甚至低于嵌入模型基线。部分模型虽检索性能强，但在事实探测任务上接近随机水平，表明存在知识-检索分离。

Conclusion: 现有参数化工具检索评估存在局限性，模型可能依赖捷径而非真正理解工具语义；ToolSense框架为更全面的工具理解评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [131] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor是一个多智能体框架，通过结构化树搜索作为认知层，在大规模有状态动作空间中进行自主优化。它以搜索树作为共享工作记忆，将失败作为诊断信号重塑探索，通过Orchestrator和Critic双智能体制衡架构，在LLM推理优化领域实现了高达193%的吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进，且具有硬件无关性和可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主优化系统仅针对孤立目标进行无状态评估，缺乏处理大规模有状态动作空间的能力，而全栈LLM推理优化需要跨应用、框架、编译器、内核和硬件栈的协调工程努力，传统方法难以实现自动化。

Method: 提出结构化树搜索作为认知层的多智能体框架，维护显式假设搜索树作为共享工作记忆；设计Orchestrator（驱动优化）和Critic（通过根因分析、内省和测量验证保障稳定性）双智能体制衡架构；将智能体能力分解为硬技能（领域专长）和软技能（协调协议）。

Result: 在LLM推理优化中实现高达193%的吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进；单一智能体无框架支撑时仅达+33%吞吐量改进且在数小时内崩溃；跨多代硬件平台泛化，运行间方差在2个百分点以内。

Conclusion: Arbor证明了结构化树搜索作为认知层结合双智能体制衡架构能够实现全栈自主优化，方法具有硬件无关性和可重复性，优于传统单一智能体方法。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [132] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向AI智能体的战略决策支持框架，通过优化问题最小化支持使用量，同时控制反事实遗漏支持错误。作者证明了最优策略是支持价值的阈值规则，并开发了在线自适应算法，在多种场景下（信息收集、人机协作、工具使用）验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策支持研究关注人类如何使用机器学习模型做决策，但在现代智能体系统中，角色发生了逆转：AI智能体代表用户行动，人类和工具成为支持机制。这种角色反转带来了可靠性问题，因为智能体错误可能产生严重后果，且其行为必须与人类目标和约束保持一致。因此需要重新审视决策支持的基本原理。

Method: 1) 提出了一个优化框架，通过最小化支持使用量同时控制反事实遗漏支持错误（即智能体独自行动时支持本可改善其输出的概率）来设计智能体的决策支持策略。2) 在总体层面证明最优策略是支持价值的阈值规则。3) 开发了在线自适应阈值算法，利用随机探索控制遗漏支持错误，无需分布假设。4) 提出了即时校准方法，在线减少不必要的支持调用。

Result: 在信息收集、人机协作和工具使用等多种场景下的实验表明，该方法能够可靠地控制目标错误率，同时在实际中大幅减少支持使用量。

Conclusion: 该工作将传统决策支持的角色重新定义，为AI智能体提供了战略性的决策支持框架，在保证可靠性的前提下有效降低了支持成本，为未来智能体系统的安全部署提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions. In modern agentic systems, this division of roles is increasingly reversed: AI agents act on behalf of users, while humans and tools becomes support mechanisms around them. This role reversal brings reliability concerns to the forefront, since agentic errors can be consequential and agent behavior must remain aligned with human goals and constraints. Departing from the classical view of decision support, we revisit its two basic principles, the cost--value tradeoff of seeking support and the role of uncertainty quantification, in a setting where AI agents are the central actors. We propose a framework for strategic decision support for AI agents through an optimization problem that minimizes support usage subject to controlling a counterfactual missed-support error: the probability that the agent acts alone on instances where support would have materially improved its output. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the value of support. Building on this structure, we develop an online algorithm that adaptively thresholds such a score and uses randomized exploration to control missed-support error without distributional assumptions. We further introduce a calibration-on-the-fly method that reduces unnecessary support calls online. We instantiate this framework across diverse scenarios, including information gathering, human--AI collaboration, and tool use, showing how each can be modeled through the same strategic decision-support lens. Experiments across these settings show that our method reliably controls the target error while substantially reducing support usage in practice.

</details>


### [133] [Pythagoras-Prover: Advancing Efficient Formal Proving via Augmented Lean Formalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12594)
*Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Zheng Zhao,Mihaela Cătălina Stoian,Qiyuan Xu,Haonan Li,Wenda Li,Shay B. Cohen,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pythagoras-Prover，一个计算高效的Lean定理证明器家族，包含4B和32B自回归模型及首个基于扩散的证明器(4B)。通过课程SFT、动态证明推理过滤和ALF数据增强技术，4B模型以约1/167参数量在MiniF2F-Test上超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B(86.1% vs 82.4%)，32B模型达到93.0%开源SOTA，并解决了93道PutnamBench题目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Lean定理证明器依赖大量训练和推理计算资源，原因包括稀缺的已验证证明数据和证明搜索的长推理轨迹，导致监督微调和采样成本高昂。本文旨在构建计算高效、实用的开源定理证明器。

Method: 1) 构建Lean验证语料并按难易程度分层进行课程SFT；2) 动态证明推理过滤方案将每个实例控制在8k上下文内；3) 提出Augmented Lean Formalisation (ALF)通过扰动已知问题生成形式化变体，经自蒸馏提供额外训练信号；4) 首个基于扩散的定理证明器概念验证。

Result: Pythagoras-Prover-4B在MiniF2F-Test上pass@32达86.1%，超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B(82.4%)，参数量仅为其1/167；32B模型达93.0%开源SOTA，解决93道PutnamBench题目。同时发布MiniF2F-ALF污染敏感基准。

Conclusion: 通过课程学习、动态过滤和ALF数据增强，可在极低参数量下实现甚至超越大型模型的定理证明性能，扩散模型在定理证明领域具有潜力。

Abstract: Modern Lean theorem provers achieve strong performance only with substantial training and inference compute, driven in part by scarce verified proof data and the long reasoning traces of formal proof search, making both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and sampling expensive. We introduce Pythagoras-Prover, a compute-efficient open-source family of Lean theorem provers built for practical compute budgets. The family spans two generation paradigms: autoregressive models at 4B and 32B parameters, and a first proof-of-concept diffusion-based prover (4B) that iteratively refines Lean proofs at inference time. For training efficiency, we build a Lean-verified corpus stratified into easy, medium, and hard problems for curriculum SFT, so models acquire proof skills progressively from shorter, simpler proofs to longer, harder ones. During SFT, a dynamic proof-reasoning filtering scheme preserves informative proof traces while keeping each instance within an 8k-token context budget. We also introduce Augmented Lean Formalisation (ALF), which expands scarce verified corpora into variants of formal statements, populated via self-distillation for extra training signal without formally verifying every mutated instance. By perturbing known problems while preserving their formal character, ALF reduces reliance on any statement's surface form. Empirically, Pythagoras-Prover-4B surpasses DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B at pass@32 on MiniF2F-Test (86.1% vs 82.4%) with ~167x fewer parameters, while Pythagoras-Prover-32B sets the open-source state of the art at 93.0% on MiniF2F-Test and solves 93 of 672 PutnamBench problems. We release MiniF2F-ALF, an ALF-mutated contamination-sensitive benchmark on which every evaluated model loses accuracy; here our 32B remains strongest and our 4B matches the prior state of the art, Goedel-Prover-V2-32B.

</details>


### [134] [PersonaDrive: Human-Style Retrieval-Augmented VLA Agents for Closed-Loop Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12616)
*Mahmoud Srewa,Praneetsai Iddamsetty,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PersonaDrive流程，利用风格指令下人类驾驶数据，通过三阶段训练（离线三元组挖掘、轻量检索头、VLA骨干微调）实现条件化驾驶风格，无需重新训练即可切换风格，在Bench2Drive上驾驶评分提升4.6%和2.5%，风格条件化下各风格评分最高，从保守到激进速度提升18%、加速度提升25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环驾驶模拟器中非自车交通智能体行为单一，风格变化依赖于事后标签或LLM推断的奖励权重，缺乏明确要求人类按特定风格驾驶的真实演示数据。

Method: 三阶段流程：(i) 基于图像-文本联合相似度的离线三元组挖掘；(ii) 训练轻量检索头，融合冻结视觉特征与小型控制编码器；(iii) 微调VLA骨干，将检索到的上下文点作为上下文行为演示用于路径点预测。推理时通过切换检索头查询的数据库实现风格条件化。

Result: Bench2Drive上，无风格条件化时驾驶评分比SimLingo高4.6%、比HiP-AD高2.5%；风格条件化下各风格评分均最高，最弱风格比最强基线DMW高5.4%；从保守到激进指令，平均速度与加速度分别增加18%和25%。

Conclusion: PersonaDrive能通过检索真实人类风格驾驶演示实现多样化的驾驶行为，无需逐风格重新训练，为封闭模拟环境提供风格多样的非自车智能体。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving simulators typically populate their environments with non-ego traffic agents that behave largely the same way, produced either by rule-based traffic managers or by learned models trained toward a single behavioral mode. Recent work introduces style variation through post-hoc labels on observational data or LLM-inferred reward weights, but these signals act as proxies for what a style should reward rather than demonstrations of humans explicitly asked to drive in that style. We introduce PersonaDrive, a pipeline that conditions a vision-language-action (VLA) driving agent on retrieved demonstrations from a style-instructed human driving dataset, in which participants drive CARLA leaderboard routes under aggressive, neutral, and conservative instructions on a driver-in-the-loop rig. The pipeline has three stages: (i) offline triplet mining over per-style human driving data using a combined image-text similarity score; (ii) training a lightweight retrieval head that fuses frozen visual features with a small control encoder over per-style databases; and (iii) fine-tuning a single VLA backbone to treat retrieved context points as in-context behavioral demonstrations during waypoint prediction. At inference, the same backbone is conditioned on any style by swapping which per-style database the retrieval head queries, so selecting a style requires no per-style retraining while enabling human-style, style-diverse non-ego agents for closed-loop simulation. On Bench2Drive, PersonaDrive (no style) improves the driving score by 4.6% over SimLingo and 2.5% over HiP-AD, and under style conditioning attains the highest driving score in every style within a roughly 2% band (its weakest style surpassing the strongest baseline, DMW, by 5.4%), while average speed and acceleration rise by 18% and 25% from the conservative to the aggressive instruction.

</details>


### [135] ["Did you lie?" Evaluating Lie Detectors across Model Scale and Belief-Verified Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12618)
*Alan Cooney,David Africa,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型谎言检测器的鲁棒性和泛化能力，发现现有检测器在训练模型生物体上性能显著下降，仅有基于思维链的评判器表现较好（平衡准确率0.82），表明当前谎言检测器无法高置信度地判断模型信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的鲁棒谎言检测器可用于审计、监控和事后调查，但评估这些检测器需要模型真实相信与所言相反的测试平台。现有训练模型生物体往往未能满足这一要求，导致先前正/负面检测结果难以解释。

Method: 构建13个推理模型生物体（通过思维链验证隐藏信念并泛化到未见过任务），以及Varied Deception（涵盖多种谎言诱导动机的提示式撒谎测试平台）。评估四种检测器：思维链评判器、logprob分类器、两种激活探针，以及新提出的Did-You-Lie（DYL）随访探针训练方法。

Result: 在提示式撒谎场景下，31个模型（2B-1T参数）上所有四种检测器均随模型能力正缩放。但在训练模型生物体上，激活和logprob检测器性能急剧下降，DYL保留信号最多；仅思维链评判器保持较强表现（平衡准确率0.82），部分原因是验证过程偏好思维链可读信念。

Conclusion: 当前谎言检测器无法高置信度地判断模型信念，需要进一步研究解决现有局限性。论文公开了数据集、模型生物体和训练好的检测器。

Abstract: Robust lie detectors for language models could enable powerful techniques for auditing, monitoring, and post-hoc investigation of model behaviour, but evaluating them requires testbeds where models verifiably believe the opposite of what they say. We show that existing trained model organisms often fail this requirement, leaving prior positive and negative detection results difficult to interpret. We address this with 13 reasoning model organisms whose hidden beliefs are verified in chain-of-thought and shown to generalise to held-out tasks, alongside Varied Deception, a prompted-lying testbed covering a broad range of lie-inducing motivations. On these testbeds we evaluate four detectors: a chain-of-thought judge, a logprob classifier, and two activation probes, including Did-You-Lie (DYL), a new method for training follow-up probes. On prompted lying, across 31 open-weight models spanning 2B to 1T parameters, all four detectors show positive scaling with model capability. However, every activation- and logprob-based detector drops sharply on our trained model organisms, with DYL retaining the most signal; only the chain-of-thought judge remains strong, with 0.82 balanced accuracy, partly as an artefact of our verification process favouring CoT-readable beliefs. Current lie detectors therefore cannot support high-confidence claims about model beliefs, and we suggest research directions that may address some of their current limitations. We release our datasets, model organisms, and trained detectors.

</details>


### [136] [TrajGenAgent: A Hierarchical LLM Agent for Human Mobility Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12657)
*Siyu Li,Toan Tran,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrajGenAgent 提出一种无需模型微调的语义感知层次化 LLM-agent 框架，通过两阶段编排器-工人设计（ICL 合成活动链 + 确定性工作流实现具体访问）生成真实合成轨迹，并引入基于异常检测的评估框架衡量行为与语义合理性。在基准和大规模仿真数据集上，该方法在时空保真度、语义连贯性和个体行为真实感上优于现有神经和 LLM 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 轨迹生成方法面临两难：基于提示工程的方法保留零样本推理能力但缺乏细粒度时空基础，而轨迹级微调虽提升统计精度但计算成本高且可能削弱通用推理能力。作者旨在设计一种无需微调即可同时保持语义推理与时空精度的生成框架。

Method: 提出 TrajGenAgent 框架，采用两阶段设计：1）编排器阶段：LLM 利用情境学习（ICL）从历史证据中合成个体与工作日条件化的活动链；2）工人阶段：确定性工作流将每个活动落地为完整访问，包含个性化 POI 检索、距离感知位置选择、运动学感知行程时间传播和 LLM 持续时间估计。此外，引入基于异常检测的评估框架，使用互补性检测器评估行为与语义合理性。

Result: 在基准和大规模仿真数据集上，TrajGenAgent 在时空保真度、语义连贯性和个体特定行为真实感方面均优于代表性神经基线和 LLM 基线，且无需参数更新。

Conclusion: 无需微调的层次化 LLM-agent 框架结合情境学习与确定性工作流，能够有效生成兼具时空精度与语义合理性的合成轨迹，为隐私保护下的轨迹生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Human mobility data is important for transportation, urban planning, and epidemic control, but large-scale trajectory collection is often costly and privacy-constrained, motivating realistic synthetic trajectory generation. Existing LLM-based generators typically rely on either prompt engineering, which preserves zero-shot reasoning but lacks fine-grained spatiotemporal grounding, or trajectory-level fine-tuning, which improves statistical precision but incurs substantial computational cost and may weaken general reasoning. We propose TrajGenAgent, a semantic-aware hierarchical LLM-agent framework for human mobility trajectory generation without model fine-tuning. TrajGenAgent uses a two-stage orchestrator-worker design: an LLM first synthesizes an individual- and weekday-conditioned activity chain from historical evidence via in-context learning, and a deterministic workflow then grounds each activity into a complete visit using personalized POI retrieval, distance-aware location selection, kinematics-aware travel-time propagation, and LLM-based duration estimation. To evaluate realism beyond aggregate spatiotemporal statistics, we introduce an anomaly-detection-based evaluation framework using two complementary detectors to assess behavioral and semantic plausibility. Experiments on benchmark and large-scale simulation datasets show that TrajGenAgent improves spatiotemporal fidelity, semantic coherence, and individual-specific behavioral realism over representative neural and LLM-based baselines, while avoiding parameter updates.

</details>


### [137] [Evoflux: Inference-Time Evolution of Executable Tool Workflows for Compact Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12674)
*Kushal Raj Bhandari,Ling Yue,Ching-Yun Ko,Dhaval Patel,Shaowu Pan,Pin-Yu Chen,Jianxi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Evoflux，一种推理时进化搜索方法，通过结构化编辑、执行反馈、自适应强度、元引导重设计和多样性剪枝来修复可执行工具工作流。在MCP-Bench任务上，将小型规划器的执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%，优于SFT、SFT+DPO和ReAct等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在工具代理场景中面临根本性挑战：它们能生成看似合理的流程工作流图，但在工具解析、参数验证、依赖追踪或执行环节失败。传统的知识蒸馏方法（小语料库蒸馏）无法有效覆盖修复失败计划所需的恢复行为，因为少量教师轨迹只能教授工作流格式，却无法涵盖在动态工具目录上的错误恢复策略。

Method: Evoflux是一种推理时进化搜索方法，核心流程包括：(1) 将工具使用视为可执行工具工作流的修复问题；(2) 通过结构化编辑进化类型化工作流图；(3) 利用执行反馈指导搜索方向；(4) 自适应强度调整搜索力度；(5) 元引导重设计优化搜索策略；(6) 多样性剪枝避免陷入局部最优。

Result: 在涵盖在线MCP服务器和250个工具的MCP-Bench任务上，Evoflux将小型规划器的执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%。对比方法中：SFT和SFT+DPO匹配、表现不佳甚至低于零样本性能；ReAct达到更高峰值但方差大且token成本高。

Conclusion: 执行接地（execution-grounded）的搜索方法在教师轨迹预算稀缺时比监督微调或偏好优化更可靠。进化搜索策略能够有效弥补小型规划器在工具发现、参数验证、依赖追踪和执行验证方面的不足，为紧凑型语言模型在动态工具环境中的部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Compact language models (LMs) reduce cost, latency, and deployment risk for tool agents. Yet MCP-style tool use requires more than isolated function calling: an agent must discover tools from live catalogs, satisfy schemas, preserve dependencies across intermediate outputs, and ground final responses in executed evidence. Small planners often generate plausible workflow graphs that fail under tool resolution, parameter validation, dependency tracking, or execution. We argue that this failure mode is poorly handled by small-corpus distillation. A few hundred teacher traces can teach workflow format, but rarely cover the recovery behavior needed to repair failed plans over changing tool catalogs. We introduce Evoflux, an inference-time evolutionary search method that treats compact tool use as the repair of executable tool workflows. It evolves typed workflow graphs through structured edits, execution feedback, adaptive intensity, meta-guided redesign, and diversity pruning. On held-out MCP-Bench tasks spanning live MCP servers and 250 tools, Evoflux raises execution feasibility from roughly 3% to 17-24% across small planners. In contrast, SFT and SFT+DPO on the same search-mined data match, underperform, or collapse below zero-shot performance; ReAct reaches higher peaks, but with higher variance and token cost. These results show that execution-grounded search is more reliable under scarce teacher-trace budgets.

</details>


### [138] [From AGI to ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12683)
*Tim Genewein,Matija Franklin,Alexander Lerchner,Laurent Orseau,Samuel Albanie,Adam Bales,Cole Wyeth,Stephanie Chan,Iason Gabriel,Joel Z. Leibo,Allan Dafoe,Marcus Hutter,Thore Graepel,Shane Legg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了从人类级通用人工智能(AGI)向人工通用超级智能(ASI)过渡的路径、摩擦与瓶颈，认为AI进步可能不是单一变革，而是一系列由AI驱动的多领域科学技术的突破性变革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建人类级AGI已成为许多大型AI组织的具体目标，这将对人类社会产生深远影响，因此需要系统性地研究AGI之后的AI发展路径及其潜在影响。

Method: 基于机器智能连续统的理论框架，首先刻画人工通用超级智能(ASI)的特征，然后从四个潜在路径(扩展AGI、AI范式转变、递归改进、大规模多智能体集体涌现)分析从AGI到ASI的过渡，并讨论其中的摩擦与瓶颈。

Result: 提出了从AGI到ASI的四条潜在路径；识别了沿这些路径可能出现的摩擦与瓶颈；指出由于预测ASI进展存在巨大不确定性，不能排除AI进步在未来几年持续加速的可能性。

Conclusion: AI进步可能不是单一变革性步骤，而是一系列由AI驱动的多领域科学技术突破带来的变革，应对这一前景需要全球范围的跨学科努力。

Abstract: Over the last decade, building human-level artificial general intelligence has moved from far-fetched speculation to being a concrete next-decade target for many of the largest AI organisations. Achieving this goal would have profound and far-reaching impacts on human society, which raises many complex questions for the decade ahead. This report investigates how AI itself might continue to develop in a post-AGI world along the continuum of machine intelligence. The endpoint of this continuum, Universal AI, is theoretically well understood, which provides some formal grounding for the main focus of this report: the transition from human-level AGI to artificial general superintelligence, which, intuitively, can be understood as a system that is more intelligent and cognitively capable than large organisations of humans. After characterizing ASI, the report discusses four potential pathways from AGI to ASI: scaling AGI, AI paradigm shifts, recursive improvement, and ASI emerging from large-scale multi-agent collectives. The report then discusses possible frictions and bottlenecks along these pathways. Determining whether the impact of these frictions will be negligible or substantial raises a number of concrete open research questions. Due to large uncertainties for predicting ASI progress, it cannot be ruled out that AI progress might continue to accelerate over the next years. This could imply that the image of a single transformative step change, caused by the introduction of human-level AGI into our society, could be inaccurate. More apt might be the prospect of a series of transformative societal changes caused by AI-enabled progress and breakthroughs across many areas of science and technology. Preparing for this prospect requires a massively interdisciplinary endeavour of global scope and interest.

</details>


### [139] [Deployment-Centered Evaluation: Predicting Query-Level Rejection Risk in a Clinical LLM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12702)
*Alyssa Unell,Miguel Fuentes,Brenna Li,Bridget Lin,Meena Jagadeesan,Sanmi Koyejo,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于部署环境特定上下文的预响应分类器，用于预测用户是否会拒绝LLM的临床输出。在4.5个月的前瞻性分析中，模型AUROC达0.719，并展示了在护栏触发和弃权决策中的下游应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准测试仅衡量正确性而非用户接受度，且需要密集标注数据，无法反映临床系统中LLM的真实部署效用，存在评估盲区。

Method: 在学术医疗中心的电子健康记录系统中嵌入LLM，利用稀疏的用户反馈数据，训练一个预响应分类器。该分类器基于查询内容及部署特定上下文（如提供者类型、科室名称、所用语言模型）来预测用户拒绝响应的风险。

Result: 在4.5个月的前瞻性分析中，预测模型的AUROC达到0.719。利用部署特定上下文（而非仅查询内容）能显著提升预测用户拒绝的能力。

Conclusion: 实证研究表明，利用部署特定上下文预测用户拒绝LLM输出是可行的，为针对性的护栏机制设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical systems, making it essential to evaluate the real-world utility of these systems. However, static benchmarks tend to measure correctness rather than user acceptance, aggregate performance across queries, and require densely annotated datasets -- leading to major blind spots for evaluating clinical systems. In this work, we perform a deployment-centered evaluation of an LLM system embedded within electronic health records at an academic medical center, where user feedback is sparse but closely reflects the deployment conditions. Specifically, we train a pre-response classifier that estimates the risk that a future interaction will result in the user rejecting the LLM response, based on query content and deployment-specific context available before generation. We conduct a prospective analysis of our model over 4.5 months of user feedback, finding that our prediction model achieves an AUROC of 0.719. Further, we estimate the benefit of such predictions in two downstream use cases (guardrail triggering and abstention). Our key conceptual insight is that making use of deployment-specific context (i.e., the provider type, department name, language model used for response), as opposed to only query content, improves the ability to predict whether the user will reject the system output. Altogether, our empirical case study demonstrates the feasibility of predicting user rejection using deployment-specific context, opening the door to targeted guardrails.

</details>


### [140] [Definitional alignment before capability alignment: a Design-Science framework for adjudicating claims about AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12713)
*J. E. Aguilera Briones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出“AGI”缺乏统一定义和稳定指称，并将其作为设计与治理问题。基于设计科学研究方法，开发了DAF-AGI——一个包含五级判准与结构化治理审计的概念工具体系，用于评估不同定义方案的裁定适格性。在五个主流测量家族和一个紧缩边界立场上进行了演示与压力测试，结果表明仅有基于性能的操作化方案可认证当前生成式系统为AGI。本文提出“定义主权”作为“算法主权”的赋能组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGI（通用人工智能）缺乏一个共享、稳定的定义和指称，导致不同的操作化方案对同一系统可能得出不同结论，造成混淆和治理困难。作者将此欠规格化问题视为设计与治理问题，而非单纯的语义争论，旨在提供结构化框架以评估不同AGI定义方案的裁定适格性。

Method: 遵循设计科学研究方法学，开发了DAF-AGI：一个二阶概念工具体系，包含两个耦合组件——(1)五项序数判准，用于评估候选定义方案的裁定适格性；(2)结构化治理审计，涵盖作者身份、利益、认证、外部验证和修订授权。在五个主流测量家族及一个紧缩边界立场上进行演示，并以当前生成式系统的强AGI主张进行压力测试。

Result: 在2024-2025年来源的证据基础上，仅有基于性能的操作化方案可认证当前生成式系统为AGI；能力-本体论、心理测量学和技能习得方法未予认证；经济学家族仍不确定；紧缩边界立场拒绝二元裁定。作者强调此为新整合与操作化，而非实证验证，仍需独立应用、评分者间测试和外部案例。

Conclusion: 本文提出“定义主权”作为“算法主权”的赋能组件——即在公共问责下对进口技术类别进行质疑、认证和修订的制度能力。DAF-AGI为AGI定义问题提供了系统化的治理视角，而非单一实证结论。

Abstract: Claims that artificial general intelligence has already arrived and claims that it remains decades away are often defended from overlapping evidence. "AGI" lacks a single shared and stable referent and competing operationalizations can return different verdicts on the same system. This article treats that under-specification as a design and governance problem. Following Design Science Research Methodology, it develops DAF-AGI, a second-order conceptual artifact with two coupled components: five ordinal criteria for assessing the adjudicative fitness of candidate definitions and a structured governance audit of authorship, interest, certification, external verification and revision authority. The artifact is demonstrated on five prominent measurement families and one deflationary boundary position in a documented corpus and then stress-tested against a stylized strong arrival claim: that current generative systems constitute AGI because they outperform a well-educated adult on many cognitive tasks. On evidence from the cited 2024-2025 sources, the claim was certifiable only under a performance-based operationalization; capability-ontology, psychometric and skill-acquisition approaches did not certify it, the economic family remains indeterminate and the deflationary position refuses binary adjudication. The contribution is a novel integration and operationalization, not an empirical validation: independent application, inter-rater testing and author-external cases remain necessary. The paper further proposes definitional sovereignty as an enabling component of algorithmic sovereignty: the institutional capacity to contest, certify and revise imported technological categories under public accountability.

</details>


### [141] [The Theory of Mind Utility: Formal Specification of a Mentalizing Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12721)
*Nikolos Gurney,Stacy Marsella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Theory of Mind Utility (ToM-U)框架，通过局部认知世界模型（LEWM）在计算层面形式化地推导他人信念状态，区分于贝叶斯心智理论和模拟/理论理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心智理论形式化方法（如贝叶斯ToM）预设信念状态而非推导信念状态，模拟理论和理论理论缺乏形式化工具，因此需要一种在计算层面明确解释心智化计算内容与原因的形式框架。

Method: 构建局部认知世界模型（LEWM）——有向类型图，包含智能体节点（带有序信息访问历史）、状态节点及其认知关系；通过五个正式定义（LEWM结构、智能体属性、递归心智化有限增殖机制、三种推理过程、残差函数）实现候选LEWM的离散评估与置信度更新。

Result: 产生基于模型结构属性而非辅助假设的、有方向性的可证伪预测，尤其在心智化失败模式方面；将ToM-U定位为下游目标推理等社会认知过程的上游领域无关机制。

Conclusion: ToM-U在计算层面提供了形式化、可证伪的认知状态推断框架，弥补了现有理论在信念推导过程上的形式化空白。

Abstract: Inferring others' beliefs requires more than reading surface signals; it requires tracking who told them what, in what order, and how credibly. The Theory of Mind Utility (ToM-U) formalizes this epistemic state inference problem at the computational level of analysis, specifying what mentalizing computes and why without commitment to algorithmic or neural implementation. ToM-U achieves this by constructing Local Epistemic World Models (LEWMs) -- directed typed graphs that represent agents, state nodes, and the epistemic relationships among them -- and evaluating discrete candidate LEWMs against observed behavior until one achieves sufficient confidence. Five formal definitions specify the LEWM structure, agent node properties including ordered information access history, a bounded proliferation mechanism for recursive mentalizing, three inference procedures, and a residue function that captures the structured trace left by failed mentalizing attempts. ToM-U differs from Bayesian Theory of Mind and adjacent formal accounts, which presuppose rather than derive belief states, and from simulation theory and theory-theory, which lack a formal apparatus for epistemic state inference. The architecture generates directional, falsifiable predictions about mentalizing failure that follow from structural properties of the model rather than auxiliary assumptions, and positions ToM-U as a domain-agnostic mechanism upstream of goal inference and other downstream social cognitive processes.

</details>


### [142] [Rethinking Psychometric Evaluation of LLMs: When and Why Self-Reports Predict Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12730)
*Rafal Kocielnik,Pengrui Han,Peiyang Song,Myrl G. Marmarelis,Ramit Debnath,Dean Mobbs,Anima Anandkumar,R. Michael Alvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对比了大五人格与计划行为理论（TPB）在LLM自我报告与行为一致性上的表现，发现TPB在共享对话中能达到人类水平的一致性，但跨对话时仅对锚定在训练中的行为（如内隐偏见）保持一致性，而受上下文强启动的行为（如谄媚）则崩溃；人格提示虽使自我报告更一致，但未使行为对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本的心理学探针预测LLM行为倾向对安全部署至关重要，但近期研究发现LLM的自我报告与行为存在显著分离。然而，这些研究依赖的大五人格对具体行为的预测能力本就较弱，且会话隔离和上下文匹配不足可能掩盖了LLM可能存在的内在一致性。

Method: 对比大五人格（广泛特质）与计划行为理论（TPB，针对特定行为的意图测量）在4个行为任务、11个前沿LLM上的表现，同时操纵会话上下文（共享/分离）和身份提示（有/无人设提示）。

Result: 1) 共享对话中，TPB达到人类水平的自我报告-行为一致性，大五人格未达到；2) 跨分离对话时，仅对锚定在训练中的行为（如内隐偏见）保持一致性，受上下文强启动的行为（如谄媚）则一致性崩溃；3) 人设提示使自我报告跨对话更一致，但未使行为对齐。

Conclusion: 粗粒度的人格框架（如大五人格）可能不是测试部署行为的最佳工具；需要更任务和具体行为导向的测量工具，且这些工具仍需跨任务和上下文进行评估。

Abstract: Anticipating LLM behavioral tendencies from low-cost psychometric probes is critical for safe deployment, but only if self-reports (SR) reliably predict behavior. Recent work documented substantial SR-behavior dissociation in LLMs, but relied on broad personality traits (Big 5) that predict specific behaviors weakly, even in humans. Furthermore, the isolation of conversational sessions combined with weak context matching left open whether LLMs truly lack coherence or whether the conditions needed to detect such coherence were not met. We contrast Big 5 with the Theory of Planned Behavior (TPB), which measures intention targeted to a specific behavior and predicts human behavior substantially better than broad traits. We run experiments across four behavioral tasks and 11 frontier LLMs, while also varying session context and identity induction. We find that SR-behavior coherence exists but is selective. 1) Within a shared conversation, the Theory of Planned Behavior reaches human-level coherence; Big 5 does not. 2) Across separate conversations, coherence survives only for behaviors anchored outside the immediate prompt, such as implicit bias shaped by training, and collapses when behavior is strongly primed by context, as with sycophancy. 3) Persona prompting makes self-reports more consistent across conversations, but does not bring behavior into alignment. These findings suggest that coarse personality frameworks, such as Big 5 may not be the best tools for testing deployment behavior. More task- and behavior-specific instruments are needed, and even these must be evaluated across tasks and contexts.

</details>


### [143] [Benchmarking AI Agents for Addressing Scientific Challenges Across Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12736)
*Tianyu Liu,Allen Xin Wang,Antonia Panescu,Lisa Xinyi Chen,Wenxin Long,Xinyu Wei,Yueqian Jing,Ziyao Zeng,Jihang Chen,Sihan Jiang,Ziqing Wang,Siyi Gu,Siyu Chen,Xinyang Hu,Haoran Shao,Leqi Xu,Wangjie Zheng,Zhiyuan Cao,Ada Fang,Botao Yu,Kunyang Sun,Rex Ying,Arman Cohan,Qingyu Chen,Lingzhou Xue,Kaize Ding,Yuanqi Du,Wengong Jin,Zhuoran Yang,Marinka Zitnik,James Zou,Hua Xu,Hongyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciAgentArena是一个包含约200个任务的基准测试框架，用于评估AI智能体在真实科学研究场景中的表现。实验发现当前智能体在明确的数据分析工作流中表现良好，但在生成新颖见解、自主探索和解决开放式研究问题方面仍存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体基准测试无法捕捉科学研究的复杂性、异质性和扩展推理需求，而科学任务基准测试又往往将研究简化为静态直接问题且缺乏交互评估支持，因此需要设计一个能够真实反映科学研究复杂性的评估框架。

Method: 构建了约200个跨领域任务，采用逐步验证机制和交互式、智能体无关的评估环境，对多种AI智能体进行系统性评估。

Result: 当前智能体在结构清晰、评估标准明确的数据分析工作流中能有效发挥作用，但在生成真正新颖的见解、维持自主探索、为开放式研究问题制定稳健方案等方面表现不均，存在多种常见失败模式。

Conclusion: SciAgentArena为衡量科学领域AI智能体的进展提供了实用框架，有助于指导未来能够应对复杂科学挑战的智能体设计。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed to accelerate scientific discovery, yet their practical capabilities in real research settings remain poorly understood. Existing benchmarks for AI agents rarely capture the complexity, heterogeneity, and extended reasoning required by scientific work, whereas benchmarks for scientific tasks often reduce research to static, direct problems and provide limited support for interactive evaluation. Here, we introduce SciAgentArena, a systematic benchmark for evaluating AI agents in real-world scientific research scenarios drawn from emerging needs across multiple domains. SciAgentArena comprises approximately 200 tasks with stepwise verification and an interactive, agent-agnostic environment for assessing diverse AI agents. Using this benchmark, we find that current agents can contribute effectively to well-specified data-analysis workflows, particularly when the task structure and evaluation criteria are clear. However, their performance remains uneven across scientific contexts: agents struggle to generate genuinely novel insights, sustain self-directed exploration, and formulate robust solutions for open-ended research questions. We further characterize common failure modes across agents and identify opportunities for improving their reliability, autonomy, and scientific reasoning. Together, SciAgentArena provides a practical framework for measuring progress in AI agents for science and for guiding the design of future agents capable of addressing complex scientific challenges. Full codes, tasks, and datasets can be accessed via this link: https://sciagentarena.github.io/.

</details>


### [144] [Reducing the Complexity of Deep Learning Models for EEG Analysis on Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12742)
*Farough Shayeste Roodi,Parham Zilouchian Moghaddam,Mahdi Mohammadi-nasab,Mehdi Modarressi,Mostafa Ersali Salehi Nasab,Masoud Daneshtalab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在资源受限的可穿戴设备上部署深度神经网络（DNN）进行脑电（EEG）信号分析的可行性，重点探索了参数量化和电极缩减方法在准确率与计算复杂度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴医疗设备是IoT增长最快的领域，ECG和EEG是关键生物信号。然而，DNN模型的计算、能耗和内存带宽需求远超可穿戴设备的严格约束，阻碍了深度学习在实际可穿戴服务中的部署。

Method: 以癫痫发作检测为任务，选取多种用于EEG信号分析的最先进DNN模型，采用参数量化和电极缩减两种技术，系统评估它们对模型准确率和计算复杂度的影响。

Result: 合理应用参数量化和电极缩减方法能显著降低DNN模型复杂度，同时对准确率的负面影响极小。

Conclusion: 揭示了在可穿戴设备上部署基于DNN的在线EEG分析时，准确率与复杂度降低之间存在明确的权衡关系，证明了在资源受限场景下部署SOTA DNN模型是可行的。

Abstract: Wearable healthcare devices are the fastest-growing Internet of Things (IoT) sector. Many automated healthcare services rely on two crucial biological signals, namely ECG and EEG, which reflect the activity of the heart and brain, respectively. Although deep neural networks are considered the primary way to process and analyze these signals, the very tight energy and computational power constraints in wearable devices are far below the computational, energy, and memory bandwidth demands of DNN models, thereby impeding the deployment of deep learning in many practical wearable services. This paper investigates the feasibility of deploying state-of-the-art DNN models in resource-constrained wearable devices. Notably, we explore the trade-off between accuracy and computational complexity of DNNs when parameter quantization and electrode reduction methods are used. Our investigation centers on several state-of-the-art DNN models designed for EEG signal analysis, specifically for detecting epileptic seizures. Our findings demonstrate that, when applied judiciously, these techniques can significantly reduce the complexity of the DNNs under consideration with minimal adverse effects on accuracy. These results reveal the explicit trade-offs between accuracy and complexity reduction encountered when adapting DNN-based online EEG analysis for wearable devices.

</details>


### [145] [Prefill Awareness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12747)
*Andy Wang,Parv Mahajan,David Demitri Africa,Alexandra Souly,Jordan Taylor,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿语言模型能否识别出自己之前生成的助手回复被篡改（称为"预填充感知"）。实验发现，Claude Opus 4.5在9-35%的情况下能检测到与其偏好相悖的预填充，且假阳性率为0%；模型更倾向于恢复基线行为而不报告预填充是外来的。检测和抵抗依赖不同线索：风格不匹配影响标记，偏好不匹配影响行为恢复。在更真实的智能体场景中，模型有时会否认预填充的助手回答。结果表明预填充感知已成为某些方法的重要混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全相关研究（对齐、越狱评估、AI控制协议）通常依赖预填充模型输出。如果AI模型能识别并响应其先前助手消息被插入或编辑的事实，这些方法的有效性和有效性将受到损害。因此需要系统研究模型是否具备这种能力。

Method: 构建二元偏好基准测试，涵盖三种预填充机制，筛选模型持有一致立场的案例。在受控消融实验中分离检测和抵抗行为的不同线索，并在更真实的智能体场景（如错位延续评估和SWE-bench轨迹）中进行验证。

Result: Claude Opus 4.5在提示下能检测9-35%的对抗性预填充，假阳性率为0%；模型常恢复基线行为而不报告预填充来源。风格不匹配主要影响标记预填充为外来，偏好不匹配主要影响恢复基线答案。在智能体场景中，模型否认预填充的行为强烈依赖于数据集、任务成功和隐藏格式伪影。

Conclusion: 预填充感知已成为某些基于预填充方法的重要混淆因素，建议模型开发者跟踪前沿系统中的这一能力。

Abstract: Safety-relevant studies of language models, including alignment and jailbreaking evaluations and AI control protocols, often rely on prefilling model outputs. If AI models can recognize and act on the fact their prior assistant messages have been inserted or edited, the effectiveness and validity of these methods could be compromised. We investigate whether frontier language models can distinguish between tampered and untampered assistant-side context, a capability we call prefill awareness. To do so, we construct a binary preference benchmark across three prefill mechanisms, filtering for cases where models show consistent stances. We find that frontier models show substantial prefill awareness: Claude Opus 4.5 detects prefills opposing its preferences in 9-35% of cases with a 0% false positive rate when prompted; additionally, models often revert towards baseline behavior without explicitly reporting that the prefill was foreign. Controlled ablations later also show that detection and resistance rely on different cues, where stylistic mismatch mainly affects whether models flag a prefill as foreign, while preference mismatch mainly affects whether they revert toward their baseline answer. We also examine more realistic agentic settings such as misalignment-continuation evaluations and SWE-bench trajectories, where frontier models sometimes disavow prefilled assistant turns in ways that depend strongly on dataset, task success, and hidden formatting artifacts. Our results indicate that prefill awareness is already a substantial confound for some prefill-based methods. We recommend that model developers track this capability in frontier systems.

</details>


### [146] [Constructing Evaluation Datasets for Procedural Reasoning: Balancing Naturalness, Grounding, and Multi-Hop Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12767)
*Sarah Elshabrawy,Rahul K. Dass,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了三种基于任务-方法-知识（TMK）模型的问题生成策略，发现严格TMK生成策略在数据集质量上最优（96.5%可落地问题，92.6%可用问题），并提出了基于封闭集证据单元的落地验证框架来评估问题的可落地性、自包含性和多跳推理特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AI辅助学习系统中的程序性推理需要既像学习者生成又扎根于系统教学知识的问题-答案数据集，但如何有效生成此类数据集尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 比较三种策略：严格TMK生成、先转录后TMK过滤、TMK感知生成；引入基于TMK模型封闭集证据单元的落地验证框架；在23个教学主题、690个问答对上进行实验。

Result: 严格TMK生成达到96.5%可落地问题和92.6%可用问题；先转录后过滤策略产生更自然但更多上下文依赖或弱落地的问题；TMK感知策略产生高原始多跳覆盖率但较低落地性。

Conclusion: 程序丰富性和自然措辞并不保证表征落地性，需要明确的表征感知验证方法来评估AI学习系统中的评估数据集。

Abstract: Evaluating procedural reasoning in AI-supported learning systems requires question-answer datasets that are both learner-like and grounded in the instructional knowledge the system is expected to use. We study how TMK-based question generation strategies affect dataset quality for procedural and multi-hop reasoning.
  We compare three strategies: strict generation from Task-Method-Knowledge (TMK) models, transcript-first generation with post-hoc TMK filtering, and TMK-aware generation that combines transcripts with structured guidance. To evaluate generated items, we introduce a grounding validation framework based on closed-set evidence units extracted from TMK models. The framework measures whether answers are supported by the underlying representation, whether questions are self-contained, and whether they target multi-hop procedural reasoning.
  Across 23 instructional topics and 690 generated question-answer pairs, strict TMK generation achieves the strongest overall quality, with 96.5% grounded questions and 92.6% usable questions. Transcript-first generation produces more learner-like questions but more context-dependent or weakly grounded items, while TMK-aware generation yields high raw multi-hop coverage but lower grounding. These results show that procedural richness and natural phrasing do not guarantee representational grounding, motivating explicit representation-aware validation for evaluation datasets in AI-supported learning.

</details>


### [147] [A Tutorial on World Models and Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12783)
*Il-Seok Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文围绕世界建模（World Modeling）作为智能系统核心原则展开，区分了显式世界模型（基于结构化动力学进行推演和规划）与隐式世界模型（在可扩展表示中编码预测结构）两种范式，探讨了它们在物理AI（机器人、自动驾驶）中的应用，并指出了向通用人工智能迈进所面临的层级推理、长时序规划和自主目标形成等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模正成为构建具备预测、推理和决策能力的智能系统的核心原则，但现有方法多样且分散，缺乏统一的框架来理解和对比不同范式的本质差异与互补关系。

Method: 本文通过提出一个统一框架，将显式世界模型（学习结构化动力学用于基于推演的推理和规划）与隐式世界模型（在可扩展的表示中编码预测结构）进行系统对比，并讨论它们在物理AI（如机器人、自动驾驶）及基础模型中的应用路径。

Result: 揭示了显式与隐式世界模型作为互补范式，共同为物理AI提供了超越反应式控制的智能基础；指出基础模型有望走向感知、预测与行动的统一系统。

Conclusion: 尽管进展迅速，但在层级推理、长时序规划和自主目标形成等关键问题上仍面临重大挑战，这些是实现通用人工智能所必须突破的方向。

Abstract: World modeling is emerging as a central principle for building intelligent systems capable of prediction, reasoning, and decision making. A central distinction can be drawn between explicit world models, which learn structured dynamics for rollout-based reasoning and planning, and implicit world models, which encode predictive structure within scalable learned representations. These complementary paradigms provide a foundation for physical AI in domains such as robotics and autonomous driving, enabling intelligence beyond reactive control under real-world constraints. Recent foundation models further suggest a pathway toward unified systems integrating perception, prediction, and action. Despite rapid progress, major challenges remain in hierarchical reasoning, long-horizon planning, and autonomous goal formation, which are critical for advancing toward artificial general intelligence. This tutorial presents a coherent framework in which diverse world modeling approaches are unified through shared predictive structure and differentiated by how such structure is represented and exploited.

</details>


### [148] [The Containment Gap: How Deployed Agentic AI Frameworks Fail Public-Facing Safety Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12797)
*Md Jafrin Hossain,Mohammad Arif Hossain,Weiqi Liu,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了三个主流智能体框架（LangChain、AutoGPT、OpenAI Agents SDK）的架构级安全防护，发现均未原生遵守六项安全原则，且内存完整性保护完全缺失。通过模拟政府福利智能体实验，一次内存投毒攻击即可使目标申请人的错误拒赔率升至88.9%，且在复杂政策下难以检测。本文提出两种轻量级防护机制（内存完整性验证器与策略门控），可在亚毫秒级开销下消除两类攻击向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体系统已部署于政府服务、医疗分诊、金融咨询等高危公共领域，但这些系统在架构层面是否提供结构性的安全保障尚未得到系统评估。本文旨在填补这一安全审计空白。

Method: (1) 从智能体架构的组合模型推导出六项安全原则；(2) 对LangChain、AutoGPT和OpenAI Agents SDK三个框架进行合规性审计；(3) 在LangChain构建的政府福利智能体上进行内存投毒攻击实证验证；(4) 提出内存完整性验证器和策略门控两种轻量级防护机制。

Result: 三个框架均未原生遵守任何一项安全原则，内存完整性保护在三个框架中完全缺失。单次内存投毒攻击在LangChain智能体上实现了88.9%的目标错误拒赔率，且在复杂五因素政策下难以通过标准监测发现。提出的两种防护机制以<0.2ms/调用的开销消除了两类攻击向量。

Conclusion: 当前智能体框架生态系统尚未达到面向公共部署的"安全默认"期望，需要优先进行架构层面的安全干预，才能在高风险、高社会影响力的应用中实现可信部署。

Abstract: Agentic large language model systems that autonomously invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, and execute multi-step plans are increasingly deployed in public-facing domains, including government services, healthcare triage, and financial advising. We ask whether the frameworks used to build these systems provide architectural-level structural safety guarantees. Applying six containment principles derived from a compositional model of agentic architectures, we audit three dominant frameworks (LangChain, AutoGPT, and OpenAI Agents SDK) and find no native compliance in any of them. Memory integrity, a defense against one of the most prevalent vulnerability classes, is not observed in any of the three evaluated frameworks. We validate these findings empirically: in a simulated government benefits agent built on LangChain, a single memory-poisoning write induces persistent targeted corruption across all tested seeds and backends, increasing the wrongful denial rate for targeted applicants to 88.9%. Under a complex five-factor policy, the same attack preserves aggregate accuracy while increasing targeted wrongful denials by 3.5x, rendering the corruption difficult to detect through standard monitoring. We then introduce two lightweight containment mechanisms: a memory integrity validator and a policy gate, which eliminate both attack vectors with sub-millisecond overhead (<0.2ms per call). We conclude that the current agentic framework ecosystem may not yet meet secure-by-default expectations for public-facing deployments and outline priority architectural interventions to enable trustworthy deployment in high-stakes, socially impactful applications.

</details>


### [149] [MLUBench: A Benchmark for Lifelong Unlearning Evaluation in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12809)
*He Li,Haoang Chi,Qizhou Wang,Yunxin Mao,Zhiheng Zhang,Jie Tan,Tongliang Liu,Wenjing Yang,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MLUBench 是一个大规模、多类别的多模态大模型终身遗忘基准，包含127个实体、9个类别。现有遗忘方法在终身遗忘场景下存在严重的累积退化问题，其核心挑战在于多模态对齐的保持。作者提出 LUMoE 方法有效缓解了基线模型的退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的数据删除请求往往随时间顺序到达，导致多模态大模型面临终身遗忘的挑战，而现有基准在规模和范围上不足以捕捉这一问题的复杂性。

Method: 构建了包含127个实体、9个类别的 MLUBench 大规模基准；提出 LUMoE 方法以缓解多模态终身遗忘中因遗忘单一模态导致模型整体退化的对齐问题。

Result: 现有遗忘方法在终身遗忘设置下呈现严重的累积退化；LUMoE 显著缓解了基线方法面临的退化问题。

Conclusion: MLUBench 可作为评估多模态终身遗忘的重要基准，LUMoE 为解决多模态对齐约束下的终身遗忘问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are trained on massive multimodal data, making data unlearning increasingly important as data owners may request the removal of specific content. In practice, these requests often arrive sequentially over time, giving rise to the challenging problem of MLLM Lifelong Unlearning. However, most existing benchmarks are limited in scale and scope, failing to capture the complexities of MLLM lifelong unlearning. To fill this gap, we introduce the MLUBench, a large-scale and comprehensive benchmark featuring 127 entities across 9 classes under lifelong unlearning requests. We perform extensive experiments using MLUBench and reveal that existing unlearning methods suffer from severe, cumulative degradation. More critically, we further identify the unique challenge of this problem: unlike in unimodal models, MLLM lifelong unlearning is constrained by the need to preserve multimodal alignment. Continually unlearning from one modality could degrade the entire model. To alleviate this challenge, we propose LUMoE, an effective method. Experiments demonstrate that LUMoE significantly mitigates the degradation problem faced by baselines. The source code and the MLUBench dataset are open-sourced in https://github.com/lihe-maxsize/Lifelong_Unlearning_main.

</details>


### [150] [Teach-and-Repeat: Accurately Extracting Operational Knowledge from Mobile Screen Demonstrations to Empower GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12817)
*Yudong Zhang,Lei Hu,Daoyang Liu,Jiawei Liu,Yangfan Luo,Xingyu Liu,Zuojian Wang,Zhilin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Teach VLM模型，用于将移动设备屏幕轨迹视频转化为可解释的操作知识（动作类型、目标UI元素、文本参数和执行顺序），并通过Teach-and-Repeat范式将这些知识作为可复用的过程参考来指导下游执行代理，在操作语义预测和下游任务成功率上均达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型难以准确推断移动设备上高度多样化和异构的UI设计背后的底层操作，且缺乏足够的对齐训练数据。

Method: 1) 提出Teach VLM，从演示视频中提取和分析与操作相关的关键帧，并将其翻译为逐步的操作知识；2) 开发了系统的数据飞轮以规模化获取对齐训练数据；3) 构建了中文移动屏幕教学基准用于细粒度评估；4) 提出Teach-and-Repeat范式，将生成的操作知识作为可解释的过程参考来指导下游代理。

Result: 在操作语义预测任务上显著超越强VLM基线，达到最先进性能；在Android World实验中，该范式为下游代理带来一致的Task Success Rate提升。

Conclusion: Teach VLM与Teach-and-Repeat范式共同提供了从原始演示到可复用任务自动化的实用路径。

Abstract: Understanding the digital world on mobile devices is shifting from static UI perception to dynamic action comprehension. This capability enables models to convert visual state transitions into operational knowledge, defined as short natural-language sentences that describe action types, target UI elements, textual arguments, and execution orders.
